   38: def _clean(value):
   39:     if value is None:
   40:         return None
   41: 
   42:     if isinstance(value, str):
   43:         value = value.strip()
   44:         return value or None
   45: 
   46:     return value
   47: 
   48: 
   49: def _json_safe(value):
   50:     if value is None:
   51:         return None
   52: 
   53:     if isinstance(value, dict):
   54:         return {
   55:             str(key): _json_safe(item)
   56:             for key, item in value.items()
   57:         }
   58: 
   59:     if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
   60:         return [
   61:             _json_safe(item)
   62:             for item in value
   63:         ]
   64: 
   65:     if isinstance(
   66:         value,
   67:         (
   68:             UUID,
   69:             Decimal,
   70:             datetime,
   71:             date,
   72:         ),
   73:     ):
   74:         return str(value)
   75: 
   76:     return value
   77:

   69:             Decimal,

   29: def _mask_email(email: str | None) -> str | None:
   30:     if not email or "@" not in email:
   31:         return None
   32: 
   33:     local, domain = email.split("@", 1)
   34: 
   35:     if len(local) <= 1:
   36:         masked_local = "*"
   37:     elif len(local) == 2:
   38:         masked_local = f"{local[0]}*"
   39:     else:
   40:         masked_local = (
   41:             local[0]
   42:             + ("*" * min(len(local) - 2, 8))
   43:             + local[-1]
   44:         )
   45: 
   46:     return f"{masked_local}@{domain}"
   47:

   49: def verify_public_application_identity(

   51:     national_id: str,

   53:     student_number = (
   54:         student_number.strip().upper()
   55:     )

   64:         raise ValueError(

   72:                 SELECT
   73:                     id,
   74:                     student_number,
   75:                     national_id,

   77:                     application_cycle,
   78:                     app_status,

   81:                     email,

   83:                     created_at,
   84:                     updated_at

   86:                 WHERE
   87:                     student_number = :student_number
   88:                     AND TRIM(national_id) = :national_id
   89:                 LIMIT 1
   90:                 """
   91:             ),

   96:         ).mappings().first()

   99:         raise ValueError(
  100:             "Student number and ID number do not "
  101:             "match our application records."
  102:         )

  260: def get_public_application_catalogue() -> dict:
  261:     query = text(
  262:         """
  263:         SELECT
  264:             cy.cycle_code,
  265:             cy.cycle_name,
  266:             cy.application_start_date,
  267:             cy.application_end_date,
  268:             cy.program_start_date,
  269:             cy.expected_completion_date,
  270:             cy.cipc_required,
  271: 
  272:             c.course_code,
  273:             c.course_name,
  274:             c.qualification_type,
  275:             c.nqf_level,
  276:             c.credits,
  277:             c.completion_months,
  278:             c.entry_requirements,
  279:             c.assessment_type,
  280:             c.sdp_code
  281: 
  282:         FROM public.cycles cy
  283: 
  284:         JOIN public.cycle_courses cc
  285:             ON cc.cycle_id = cy.id
  286:             AND cc.is_active = true
  287: 
  288:         JOIN public.courses c
  289:             ON c.course_code = cc.course_code
  290:             AND c.status = 'Active'
  291: 
  292:         WHERE
  293:             cy.status = 'Active'
  294:             AND (
  295:                 cy.application_start_date IS NULL
  296:                 OR cy.application_start_date <= CURRENT_DATE
  297:             )
  298:             AND (
  299:                 cy.application_end_date IS NULL
  300:                 OR cy.application_end_date >= CURRENT_DATE
  301:             )
  302: 
  303:         ORDER BY
  304:             cy.program_start_date,
  305:             cy.cycle_name,
  306:             c.course_name
  307:         """
  308:     )
  309: 
  310:     with engine.connect() as connection:
  311:         rows = connection.execute(
  312:             query
  313:         ).mappings().all()
  314: 
  315:     cycles_by_code: dict[str, dict] = {}
  316: 
  317:     for row in rows:
  318:         item = dict(row)
  319: 
  320:         cycle_code = item["cycle_code"]
  321: 
  322:         cycle = cycles_by_code.setdefault(
  323:             cycle_code,
  324:             {
  325:                 "cycle_code": cycle_code,
  326:                 "cycle_name": item.get("cycle_name"),
  327:                 "application_start_date": item.get(
  328:                     "application_start_date"
  329:                 ),

  331:                     "application_end_date"
  332:                 ),

  334:                     "program_start_date"
  335:                 ),

  353:                 "nqf_level": item.get("nqf_level"),
  354:                 "credits": item.get("credits"),

  357:                 ),
  358:                 "entry_requirements": item.get(
  359:                     "entry_requirements"
  360:                 ),
  361:                 "assessment_type": item.get(
  362:                     "assessment_type"
  363:                 ),
  364:                 "sdp_code": item.get("sdp_code"),

  366:         )
  367: 
  368:     return {
  369:         "cycles": list(

  375: def enrich_public_application_payload(
  376:     application_data: dict,
  377: ) -> dict:
  378:     payload = dict(application_data)
  379: 
  380:     course_code = (
  381:         payload.get("qualification_id")
  382:         or ""
  383:     ).strip()
  384: 
  385:     cycle_code = (
  386:         payload.get("application_cycle")
  387:         or ""
  388:     ).strip()
  389: 
  390:     if not course_code:
  391:         raise ValueError(
  392:             "A programme must be selected."
  393:         )
  394: 
  395:     params = {
  396:         "course_code": course_code,
  397:     }
  398: 
  399:     cycle_filter = ""
  400: 
  401:     if cycle_code:
  402:         cycle_filter = (
  403:             "AND cy.cycle_code = :cycle_code"
  404:         )
  405:         params["cycle_code"] = cycle_code
  406: 
  407:     query = text(
  408:         f"""
  409:         SELECT
  410:             c.course_code,
  411:             c.course_name,
  412:             c.nqf_level,
  413:             c.credits,
  414:             c.sdp_code,
  415: 
  416:             cy.cycle_code,
  417:             cy.cycle_name,
  418:             cy.expected_completion_date
  419: 
  420:         FROM public.courses c
  421: 
  422:         JOIN public.cycle_courses cc
  423:             ON cc.course_code = c.course_code
  424:             AND cc.is_active = true
  425: 
  426:         JOIN public.cycles cy
  427:             ON cy.id = cc.cycle_id
  428: 
  429:         WHERE
  430:             c.course_code = :course_code
  431:             AND c.status = 'Active'
  432:             AND cy.status = 'Active'
  433:             AND (
  434:                 cy.application_start_date IS NULL
  435:                 OR cy.application_start_date <= CURRENT_DATE
  436:             )
  437:             AND (
  438:                 cy.application_end_date IS NULL
  439:                 OR cy.application_end_date >= CURRENT_DATE
  440:             )
  441:             {cycle_filter}
  442: 
  443:         ORDER BY cy.program_start_date
  444:         """
  445:     )
  446: 
  447:     with engine.connect() as connection:
  448:         rows = connection.execute(
  449:             query,
  450:             params,

  131:         RETURNING id
  132:     """)
